*** Add File: /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-codex-lab/.codex/WORKFLOW.md
+# Codex Working Rules
+
+This project is set up for small, reviewable Godot changes.
+
+## Default Loop
+
+1. Inspect the scene or script before editing.
+2. Keep gameplay, UI, and asset changes in separate commits when practical.
+3. Prefer Godot resource paths such as `res://scenes/main.tscn`.
+4. After code changes, run the project or at least the headless script check.
+5. Use Git status before and after changes so user edits are not overwritten.
+
+## Boundaries
+
+- Do not edit `.godot/` generated cache files.
+- Do not commit build exports unless explicitly requested.
+- Keep generated assets in `assets/` with provenance notes when added.
*** Add File: /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-codex-lab/README.md
+# Godot Codex Lab
+
+Minimal workspace for a `Godot 4 + VSCode + Codex + Git` workflow.
+
+## What This Gives Us
+
+- `project.godot`: Godot 4 project entry.
+- `scenes/main.tscn`: first runnable scene.
+- `scripts/main.gd`: first GDScript file for Codex edits.
+- `.vscode/`: editor settings, recommended extension, and run/check tasks.
+- `.codex/WORKFLOW.md`: collaboration rules for Codex changes.
+- `.gitignore`: ignores Godot cache, builds, and local scratch files.
+
+## Local Setup
+
+1. Install Godot 4 from the official site or put `Godot.app` in `/Applications`.
+2. Install VSCode and the `godot-tools` extension.
+3. In Godot, open this folder as an existing project.
+4. In Godot editor settings, enable the language server on port `6005`.
+5. In VSCode, open this folder and use `Terminal > Run Task > Godot: Run Project`.
+
+If Godot is installed elsewhere, update `.vscode/settings.json`.
+
+## Git Loop
+
+```bash
+git status
+git add .
+git commit -m "Set up Godot Codex workflow"
+```
+
+Use small commits: one feature, one fix, or one asset pipeline change at a time.

*** Add File: /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-codex-lab/.codex/WORKFLOW.md
+# Codex Working Rules
+
+This project is set up for small, reviewable Godot changes.
+
+## Default Loop
+
+1. Inspect the scene or script before editing.
+2. Keep gameplay, UI, and asset changes in separate commits when practical.
+3. Prefer Godot resource paths such as `res://scenes/main.tscn`.
+4. After code changes, run the project or at least the headless script check.
+5. Use Git status before and after changes so user edits are not overwritten.
+
+## Boundaries
+
+- Do not edit `.godot/` generated cache files.
+- Do not commit build exports unless explicitly requested.
+- Keep generated assets in `assets/` with provenance notes when added.
*** Add File: /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-codex-lab/README.md
+# Godot Codex Lab
+
+Minimal workspace for a `Godot 4 + VSCode + Codex + Git` workflow.
+
+## What This Gives Us
+
+- `project.godot`: Godot 4 project entry.
+- `scenes/main.tscn`: first runnable scene.
+- `scripts/main.gd`: first GDScript file for Codex edits.
+- `.vscode/`: editor settings, recommended extension, and run/check tasks.
+- `.codex/WORKFLOW.md`: collaboration rules for Codex changes.
+- `.gitignore`: ignores Godot cache, builds, and local scratch files.
+
+## Local Setup
+
+1. Install Godot 4 from the official site or put `Godot.app` in `/Applications`.
+2. Install VSCode and the `godot-tools` extension.
+3. In Godot, open this folder as an existing project.
+4. In Godot editor settings, enable the language server on port `6005`.
+5. In VSCode, open this folder and use `Terminal > Run Task > Godot: Run Project`.
+
+If Godot is installed elsewhere, update `.vscode/settings.json`.
+
+## Git Loop
+
+```bash
+git status
+git add .
+git commit -m "Set up Godot Codex workflow"
+```
+
+Use small commits: one feature, one fix, or one asset pipeline change at a time.
*** End Patch

*** Add File: /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-codex-lab/.codex/WORKFLOW.md
+# Codex Working Rules
+
+This project is set up for small, reviewable Godot changes.
+
+## Default Loop
+
+1. Inspect the scene or script before editing.
+2. Keep gameplay, UI, and asset changes in separate commits when practical.
+3. Prefer Godot resource paths such as `res://scenes/main.tscn`.
+4. After code changes, run the project or at least the headless script check.
+5. Use Git status before and after changes so user edits are not overwritten.
+
+## Boundaries
+
+- Do not edit `.godot/` generated cache files.
+- Do not commit build exports unless explicitly requested.
+- Keep generated assets in `assets/` with provenance notes when added.
*** Add File: /Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/godot-codex-lab/README.md
+# Godot Codex Lab
+
+Minimal workspace for a `Godot 4 + VSCode + Codex + Git` workflow.
+
+## What This Gives Us
+
+- `project.godot`: Godot 4 project entry.
+- `scenes/main.tscn`: first runnable scene.
+- `scripts/main.gd`: first GDScript file for Codex edits.
+- `.vscode/`: editor settings, recommended extension, and run/check tasks.
+- `.codex/WORKFLOW.md`: collaboration rules for Codex changes.
+- `.gitignore`: ignores Godot cache, builds, and local scratch files.
+
+## Local Setup
+
+1. Install Godot 4 from the official site or put `Godot.app` in `/Applications`.
+2. Install VSCode and the `godot-tools` extension.
+3. In Godot, open this folder as an existing project.
+4. In Godot editor settings, enable the language server on port `6005`.
+5. In VSCode, open this folder and use `Terminal > Run Task > Godot: Run Project`.
+
+If Godot is installed elsewhere, update `.vscode/settings.json`.
+
+## Git Loop
+
+```bash
+git status
+git add .
+git commit -m "Set up Godot Codex workflow"
+```
+
+Use small commits: one feature, one fix, or one asset pipeline change at a time.
*** End Patch

extends Node3D

const CHARACTER_SCENE := "res://assets/kaykit_character/Mage.glb"
const DUNGEON_ASSET_DIR := "res://assets/kaykit_dungeon/"
const PLAYER_START := Vector3(-16.0, 1.2, -4.0)
const MOVE_SPEED := 6.0
const JUMP_VELOCITY := 7.5
const GRAVITY := 22.0
const ROOM_SIZE := 8.0
const ROOM_COLUMNS := 5
const ROOM_ROWS := 2
const FLOOR_SIZE := Vector2(ROOM_COLUMNS * ROOM_SIZE, ROOM_ROWS * ROOM_SIZE)
const WALL_HEIGHT := 2.8
const WALL_THICKNESS := 0.55
const CAMERA_MIN_PITCH := deg_to_rad(-75.0)
const CAMERA_MAX_PITCH := deg_to_rad(-18.0)
const CAMERA_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY := 0.006

var player: CharacterBody3D
var player_visual: Node3D
var animation_player: AnimationPlayer
var camera: Camera3D
var status_label: Label
var camera_yaw := 0.0
var camera_pitch := deg_to_rad(-62.0)
var camera_distance := 16.0
var current_animation := ""

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_input_actions()
	_build_world()
	_build_player()
	_build_hud()


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("reset"):
		_reset_player()

	_apply_player_motion(delta)
	_update_camera(delta)


func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseMotion and Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		camera_yaw -= event.relative.x * CAMERA_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY
		camera_pitch = clampf(camera_pitch - event.relative.y * CAMERA_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY, CAMERA_MIN_PITCH, CAMERA_MAX_PITCH)

	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed:
		if event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
			camera_distance = maxf(camera_distance - 0.8, 6.5)
		elif event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
			camera_distance = minf(camera_distance + 0.8, 15.0)


func _register_input_actions() -> void:
	_add_key_action("move_left", [KEY_A, KEY_LEFT])
	_add_key_action("move_right", [KEY_D, KEY_RIGHT])
	_add_key_action("move_up", [KEY_W, KEY_UP])
	_add_<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2386" />mera_pitch),
		-sin(camera_pitch),
		cos(camera_yaw) * cos(camera_pitch)
	) * camera_distance
	var camera_target := player.global_position + offset
	camera.global_position = camera.global_position.lerp(camera_target, clampf(delta * 6.0, 0.0, 1.0))
	camera.look_at(player.global_position + Vector3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), Vector3.UP)


func _reset_player() -> void:
	player.global_position = PLAYER_START
	player.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
	_play_animation("Idle")


func _update_player_animation(is_moving: bool, was_on_floor: bool) -> void:
	if not player.is_on_floor():
		if was_on_floor and player.velocity.y > 0.0:
			_play_animation("Jump_Start")
		else:
			_play_animation("Jump_Idle")
	elif is_moving:
		_play_animation("Running_A")
	else:
		_play_animation("Idle")


func _play_animation(animation_name: String) -> void:
	if not animation_player or current_animation == animation_name:
		return
	if animation_player.has_animation(animation_name):
		animation_player.play(animation_name)
		current_animation = animation_name


func _find_animation_player(root: Node) -> AnimationPlayer:
	if root is AnimationPlayer:
		return root
	for child in root.get_children():
		var found := _find_animation_player(child)
		if found:
			return found
	return null


func _room_centers() -> Array[Vector3]:
	var centers: Array[Vector3] = []
	for row in range(ROOM_ROWS):
		for column in range(ROOM_COLUMNS):
			var x := (float(column) - float(ROOM_COLUMNS - 1) * 0.5) * ROOM_SIZE
			var z := (float(row) - float(ROOM_ROWS - 1) * 0.5) * ROOM_SIZE
			centers.append(Vector3(x, 0.0, z))
	return centers


func _instantiate_asset(path: String) -> Node3D:
	var scene := load(path)
	if scene is PackedScene:
		return scene.instantiate() as Node3D
	return null


func _material(albedo: Color, roughness: float, metallic: float) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var material := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	material.albedo_color = albedo
	material.roughness = roughness
	material.metallic = metallic
	return material

extends Node3D

const CHARACTER_SCENE := "res://assets/kaykit_character/Mage.glb"
const DUNGEON_ASSET_DIR := "res://assets/kaykit_dungeon/"
const PLAYER_START := Vector3(-16.0, 1.2, -4.0)
const MOVE_SPEED := 6.0
const JUMP_VELOCITY := 7.5
const GRAVITY := 22.0
const ROOM_SIZE := 8.0
const ROOM_COLUMNS := 5
const ROOM_ROWS := 2
const FLOOR_SIZE := Vector2(ROOM_COLUMNS * ROOM_SIZE, ROOM_ROWS * ROOM_SIZE)
const WALL_HEIGHT := 2.8
const WALL_THICKNESS := 0.55
const CAMERA_MIN_PITCH := deg_to_rad(-75.0)
const CAMERA_MAX_PITCH := deg_to_rad(-18.0)
const CAMERA_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY := 0.006

var player: CharacterBody3D
var player_visual: Node3D
var animation_player: AnimationPlayer
var camera: Camera3D
var status_label: Label
var camera_yaw := 0.0
var camera_pitch := deg_to_rad(-62.0)
var camera_distance := 16.0
var current_animation := ""

func _ready() -> void:
	_register_input_actions()
	_build_world()
	_build_player()
	_build_hud()


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("reset"):
		_reset_player()

	_apply_player_motion(delta)
	_update_camera(delta)


func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseMotion and Input.is_mouse_button_pressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT):
		camera_yaw -= event.relative.x * CAMERA_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY
		camera_pitch = clampf(camera_pitch - event.relative.y * CAMERA_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY, CAMERA_MIN_PITCH, CAMERA_MAX_PITCH)

	if event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed:
		if event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
			camera_distance = maxf(camera_distance - 0.8, 6.5)
		elif event.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
			camera_distance = minf(camera_distance + 0.8, 15.0)


func _register_input_actions() -> void:
	_add_key_action("move_left", [KEY_A, KEY_LEFT])
	_add_key_action("move_right", [KEY_D, KEY_RIGHT])
	_add_key_action("move_up", [KEY_W, KEY_UP])
	_add_<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2386" />mera_pitch),
		-sin(camera_pitch),
		cos(camera_yaw) * cos(camera_pitch)
	) * camera_distance
	var camera_target := player.global_position + offset
	camera.global_position = camera.global_position.lerp(camera_target, clampf(delta * 6.0, 0.0, 1.0))
	camera.look_at(player.global_position + Vector3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), Vector3.UP)


func _reset_player() -> void:
	player.global_position = PLAYER_START
	player.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
	_play_animation("Idle")


func _update_player_animation(is_moving: bool, was_on_floor: bool) -> void:
	if not player.is_on_floor():
		if was_on_floor and player.velocity.y > 0.0:
			_play_animation("Jump_Start")
		else:
			_play_animation("Jump_Idle")
	elif is_moving:
		_play_animation("Running_A")
	else:
		_play_animation("Idle")


func _play_animation(animation_name: String) -> void:
	if not animation_player or current_animation == animation_name:
		return
	if animation_player.has_animation(animation_name):
		animation_player.play(animation_name)
		current_animation = animation_name


func _find_animation_player(root: Node) -> AnimationPlayer:
	if root is AnimationPlayer:
		return root
	for child in root.get_children():
		var found := _find_animation_player(child)
		if found:
			return found
	return null


func _room_centers() -> Array[Vector3]:
	var centers: Array[Vector3] = []
	for row in range(ROOM_ROWS):
		for column in range(ROOM_COLUMNS):
			var x := (float(column) - float(ROOM_COLUMNS - 1) * 0.5) * ROOM_SIZE
			var z := (float(row) - float(ROOM_ROWS - 1) * 0.5) * ROOM_SIZE
			centers.append(Vector3(x, 0.0, z))
	return centers


func _instantiate_asset(path: String) -> Node3D:
	var scene := load(path)
	if scene is PackedScene:
		return scene.instantiate() as Node3D
	return null


func _material(albedo: Color, roughness: float, metallic: float) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var material := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	material.albedo_color = albedo
	material.roughness = roughness
	material.metallic = metallic
	return material

+	var values: Array[float] = []
+	for column in range(ROOM_COLUMNS):

+	return values
+
+
+func _room_row_z_values() -> Array[float]:
+	var values: Array[float] = []
+	for row in range(ROOM_ROWS):
+		values.append((float(row) - float(ROOM_ROWS - 1) * 0.5) * ROOM_SIZE)
+	return values
+

[599] assistant: 已修复并重新打开游戏。

本次修复：

- 角色朝向：取消模型 180 度反向，移动时角色面朝移动方向，不再像倒退。
- 移动方向：`W` 仍按镜头前方走，`A/S/D` 相对镜头方向。
- 地牢结构：不再是空旷大平面，改为中央 `10` 个 `30m` 房间组成的地牢区。
- 房间墙体：外墙、分隔墙、门洞现在都是真实可见墙体并带碰撞。
- 地图：保留 `200 x 200` 总可行走区域，地牢房间群位于地图中心。
- 装饰：每个房间增加箱子、柱子或宝箱，火把和灯光重新布置。

验证通过并提交：

```bash
770fd49 Fix dungeon layout and character facing
```

我已经重新启动 Godot 游戏窗口。玩法仍是：

- `WASD` / 方向键：移动
- 按住鼠标右键移动：旋转视角
- 鼠标滚轮：缩放视角
- `Space`：跳跃
- `R`：重置角色位置

+extends SceneTree

+func _init() -> void:
+	var scene := load("res://assets/global_illumination/zdm2.glb")
+	if scene is PackedScene:

+func _print_meshes(node: Node, depth: int = 0) -> void:
+	if node is MeshInstance3D:

+		print("%s%s surfaces=%d material=%s" % [" ".repeat(depth * 2), node.name, surfaces, node.get_active_material(0)])
+	for child in node.get_children():
+		_print_meshes(child, depth + 1)
